\begin{lexlean}{TargetSemantics}
\useglossary{lexlean.std.nat@1.2.0}
\importmodule{TargetSyntax}
\title{Natural number addition}
\begin{semanticmodule}
inductive{
  name: Outcome,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [
    {name: value, fields: [TargetSyntax::Value, Nat]},
    {name: overflow, fields: [Nat]},
    {name: stuck, fields: []},
    {name: exhausted, fields: []}
  ]
}

inductive{
  name: Outcomes,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [
    {name: values, fields: [(List TargetSyntax::Value), Nat]},
    {name: overflow, fields: [Nat]},
    {name: stuck, fields: []},
    {name: exhausted, fields: []}
  ]
}

definition{
  name: addSteps,
  parameters: [result : Outcome, extra : Nat],
  result: Outcome,
  body: (match result
    | Outcome.value computed steps => (ctor Outcome.value computed (steps + extra))
    | Outcome.overflow steps => (ctor Outcome.overflow (steps + extra))
    | Outcome.stuck => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
    | Outcome.exhausted => (ctor Outcome.exhausted))
}

definition{
  name: lookup,
  parameters: [environment : (List (Nat * TargetSyntax::Value)), name : Nat],
  result: (Option TargetSyntax::Value),
  body: (match environment
    | List.nil => (ctor Option.none [TargetSyntax::Value])
    | List.cons entry rest => (if ((first entry) =? name) then (ctor Option.some [TargetSyntax::Value] (second entry)) else (lookup rest name))),
  recursive_argument: environment
}

definition{
  name: depth,
  parameters: [environment : (List (Nat * TargetSyntax::Value)), name : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (match environment
    | List.nil => 0
    | List.cons entry rest => (if ((first entry) =? name) then 0 else (1 + (depth rest name)))),
  recursive_argument: environment
}

definition{
  name: bindAll,
  parameters: [
    names : (List Nat),
    values : (List TargetSyntax::Value),
    environment : (List (Nat * TargetSyntax::Value))
  ],
  result: (Option (List (Nat * TargetSyntax::Value))),
  body: (match names
    | List.nil => (match values
      | List.nil => (ctor Option.some [(List (Nat * TargetSyntax::Value))] environment)
      | List.cons ignored1 ignored2 => (ctor Option.none [(List (Nat * TargetSyntax::Value))]))
    | List.cons name names2 => (match values
      | List.nil => (ctor Option.none [(List (Nat * TargetSyntax::Value))])
      | List.cons value values2 => (bindAll names2 values2 (cons (name, value) environment)))),
  recursive_argument: names
}

definition{
  name: natResult,
  parameters: [number : Nat],
  result: Outcome,
  body: (if (number <? 18446744073709551616) then (ctor Outcome.value (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.nat number) 0) else (ctor Outcome.overflow 0))
}

definition{
  name: intResult,
  parameters: [number : Int],
  result: Outcome,
  body: (match (prim checked_convert number : (Option Int64))
    | Option.none => (ctor Outcome.overflow 0)
    | Option.some bounded => (ctor Outcome.value (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.int number) 0)),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: fromOrdering,
  parameters: [order : Ordering],
  result: TargetSyntax::Order,
  body: (if (prim equal order (prim compare_bytes 0x00 0x01 : Ordering) : Bool) then (ctor TargetSyntax::Order.less) else (if (prim equal order (prim compare_bytes 0x 0x : Ordering) : Bool) then (ctor TargetSyntax::Order.same) else (ctor TargetSyntax::Order.more))),
  axioms: [propext]
}

definition{
  name: sameOrder,
  parameters: [left : TargetSyntax::Order, right : TargetSyntax::Order],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match left
    | TargetSyntax::Order.less => (match right
      | TargetSyntax::Order.less => true
      | TargetSyntax::Order.same => false
      | TargetSyntax::Order.more => false)
    | TargetSyntax::Order.same => (match right
      | TargetSyntax::Order.less => false
      | TargetSyntax::Order.same => true
      | TargetSyntax::Order.more => false)
    | TargetSyntax::Order.more => (match right
      | TargetSyntax::Order.less => false
      | TargetSyntax::Order.same => false
      | TargetSyntax::Order.more => true))
}

definition{
  name: orderOf,
  parameters: [positions : (List Nat)],
  result: TargetSyntax::Order,
  body: (match positions
    | List.nil => (ctor TargetSyntax::Order.same)
    | List.cons leading rest => (match rest
      | List.nil => (ctor TargetSyntax::Order.same)
      | List.cons ignored3 ignored4 => (if (leading =? 0) then (ctor TargetSyntax::Order.less) else (ctor TargetSyntax::Order.more))))
}

definition{
  name: orderNat,
  parameters: [leftKey : Nat, rightKey : Nat],
  result: TargetSyntax::Order,
  body: (orderOf (prim map_values (prim map_insert (prim map_insert map_literal{

    key: Nat,
    value: Nat
  } leftKey 0 : (Map Nat Nat)) rightKey 1 : (Map Nat Nat)) : (List Nat)))

          | TargetSyntax::Value.u16 ignored4416 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.u32 ignored4417 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.u64 ignored4418 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.i8 ignored4419 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.i16 ignored4420 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.i32 ignored4421 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.i64 ignored4422 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.string ignored4423 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.bytes ignored4424 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.ordering ignored4425 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.none => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.some ignored4426 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.ok ignored4427 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.error ignored4428 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.list ignored4429 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.pair ignored4430 ignored4431 => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
          | TargetSyntax::Value.adt tag fields => (match (prim index fields position : (Option TargetSyntax::Value))
            | Option.none => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
            | Option.some selected => (ctor Outcome.value selected ((innerSteps + 1) + position)))
          | TargetSyntax::Value.closure ignored4432 ignored4433 => (ctor Outcome.stuck))
        | Outcome.overflow steps4434 => (ctor Outcome.overflow (steps4434 + 1))
        | Outcome.stuck => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
        | Outcome.exhausted => (ctor Outcome.exhausted)))),
  axioms: [Classical.choice, Quot.sound, propext],
  mutual: Evaluation,
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: evalList,
  parameters: [
    fuel : Nat,
    program : TargetSyntax::Program,
    environment : (List (Nat * TargetSyntax::Value)),
    expressions : (List TargetSyntax::Expr)
  ],
  result: Outcomes,
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => (ctor Outcomes.exhausted)
    | Nat.succ remaining => (match expressions
      | List.nil => (ctor Outcomes.values (nil TargetSyntax::Value) 0)
      | List.cons head rest => (match (eval remaining program environment head)
        | Outcome.value headValue headSteps => (match (evalList remaining program environment rest)
          | Outcomes.values restValues restSteps => (ctor Outcomes.values (cons headValue restValues) (headSteps + restSteps))
          | Outcomes.overflow restOverflow => (ctor Outcomes.overflow (headSteps + restOverflow))
          | Outcomes.stuck => (ctor Outcomes.stuck)
          | Outcomes.exhausted => (ctor Outcomes.exhausted))
        | Outcome.overflow headOverflow => (ctor Outcomes.overflow headOverflow)
        | Outcome.stuck => (ctor Outcomes.stuck)
        | Outcome.exhausted => (ctor Outcomes.exhausted)))),
  axioms: [Classical.choice, Quot.sound, propext],
  mutual: Evaluation,
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: evalArms,
  parameters: [
    fuel : Nat,
    program : TargetSyntax::Program,
    environment : (List (Nat * TargetSyntax::Value)),
    scrutinee : TargetSyntax::Value,
    arms : (List TargetSyntax::Arm)
  ],
  result: Outcome,
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => (ctor Outcome.exhausted)
    | Nat.succ remaining => match{
      branches: [
        {body: (ctor Outcome.stuck), binders: [], constructor: List.nil},
        {
          body: (match var{name: first}
            | TargetSyntax::Arm.arm shape binders body => (match (destruct shape scrutinee)
              | Option.none => (addSteps (evalArms remaining program environment scrutinee rest) 1)
              | Option.some fields => (match (bindAll binders fields environment)
                | Option.none => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
                | Option.some extended => (addSteps (eval remaining program extended body) 1)))),
          binders: [first, rest],
          constructor: List.cons
        }
      ],
      scrutinee: arms
    }),
  axioms: [Classical.choice, Quot.sound, propext],
  mutual: Evaluation,
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: run,
  parameters: [
    fuel : Nat,
    program : TargetSyntax::Program,
    entry : Nat,
    arguments : (List TargetSyntax::Value)
  ],
  result: Outcome,
  body: (match (prim index (project program functions) entry : (Option TargetSyntax::Function))
    | Option.none => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
    | Option.some function => (match (bindAll (project function parameters) arguments (nil (Nat * TargetSyntax::Value)))
      | Option.none => (ctor Outcome.stuck)
      | Option.some environment => (eval fuel program environment (project function body)))),
  axioms: [Classical.choice, Quot.sound, propext]
}
\end{semanticmodule}
\end{lexlean}
